2026-01-15T09:48:37.6538384Z ##[group]Run # Note: There is currently no way to distinguish between undefined variables and empty strings in GitHub Actions.
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6539258Z [36;1m# Note: There is currently no way to distinguish between undefined variables and empty strings in GitHub Actions.[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6539912Z [36;1m# This limitation affects how we handle default values and empty inputs.[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6540434Z [36;1m# For more information, see: https://github.com/actions/runner/issues/924[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6540817Z [36;1m[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6541171Z [36;1m# The following logic implements the configuration priority described in the README:[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6541597Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6541770Z [36;1m# Inputs[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6541978Z [36;1m# Environment Variables[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6542219Z [36;1m# Config File[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6542421Z [36;1m# Defaults[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6542602Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6542995Z [36;1m# As noted above defaults are awkward to handle as GitHub Actions will inject those values as the input[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6543659Z [36;1m# if the caller doesn't provide them, thus if the input matches the default we don't set it as we[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6544320Z [36;1m# can't tell the difference.  Plus if we did set it when it was the default value then it could potentially [0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6545050Z [36;1m# override an external environment variable, or something in the callers configuration file, which then wouldn't [0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6545817Z [36;1m# match the configuration priority that is documented.[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6546167Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided() {[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6546420Z [36;1m  local var_name="$1"[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6546661Z [36;1m  local input_value="$2"[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6546910Z [36;1m  local default_value="$3"[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6547146Z [36;1m[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6547421Z [36;1m  if [ ! -z "$input_value" ] && [ "$input_value" != "$default_value" ]; then[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6547889Z [36;1m    # If action was provided with explicit input by the caller set that[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6548327Z [36;1m    echo "export $var_name=$input_value" >> trivy_envs.txt[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6548639Z [36;1m  fi[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6548813Z [36;1m}[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6548980Z [36;1m[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6549249Z [36;1m# Set environment variables, handling those with default values[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6549799Z [36;1m# cf. https://aquasecurity.github.io/trivy/latest/docs/configuration/#environment-variables[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6550485Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_INPUT" "" ""[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6550842Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_EXIT_CODE" "1" ""[0m

2026-01-15T09:48:37.6551811Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_PKG_TYPES" "os,library" "os,library"[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6552358Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_SEVERITY" "CRITICAL,HIGH" "UNKNOWN,LOW,MEDIUM,HIGH,CRITICAL"[0m
2026-01-15T09:48:37.6552878Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_FORMAT" "table" "table"[0m
